{"forms":[{"form":"excepts","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"excepting","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"excepted","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"excepted","tags":["past"]},{"form":"excepte","tags":["alternative","archaic","rare"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"I find most people annoying — present company excepted, of course!","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To exclude; to specify as being an exception."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"to except to a witness or his testimony","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To take exception, to object (to or against)."],"tags":["intransitive"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ɪkˈsɛpt/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ɛkˈsɛpt/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ɪkˈsɛpt/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/ɛkˈsɛpt/","tags":["General-American","sometimes"]},{"ipa":"/əkˈsɛpt/"}],"upstream_line":236309,"upstream_offset":1161282043,"upstream_record_sha256":"282c379ab9a59c5d05fb0f663058fe0f8ba12f1f84eb71cfdd57075755dee8db","word":"except"}
{"antonyms":[{"word":"in addition to"},{"word":"for example"},{"word":"even"}],"forms":[{"form":"excepte","tags":["alternative","archaic","rare"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"prep","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"There was nothing in the cupboard except a tin of beans.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used to introduce an exception or qualification to something previously stated."],"synonyms":[{"word":"apart from"},{"word":"except for"},{"word":"outtake"},{"word":"with the exception of"}]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ɪkˈsɛpt/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ɛkˈsɛpt/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ɪkˈsɛpt/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/ɛkˈsɛpt/","tags":["General-American","sometimes"]},{"ipa":"/əkˈsɛpt/"}],"synonyms":[{"tags":["postpositional"],"word":"apart"},{"word":"apart from"},{"tags":["postpositional"],"word":"aside"},{"word":"aside from"},{"word":"bar"},{"word":"barring"},{"word":"besides"},{"word":"but"},{"word":"but for"},{"word":"except"},{"word":"except for"},{"word":"excepting"},{"word":"excluding"},{"tags":["British","archaic","dialectal"],"word":"forby"},{"word":"modulo"},{"word":"not counting"},{"tags":["idiomatic"],"word":"other than"},{"tags":["archaic"],"word":"outtake"},{"word":"save"},{"word":"save for"},{"word":"saving"},{"word":"with the exception of"}],"upstream_line":236310,"upstream_offset":1161296441,"upstream_record_sha256":"1ce85e19ff302e2dc142bf29ec618a7998a0f469531ba6c84a7939e998399584","word":"except"}
{"forms":[{"form":"excepte","tags":["alternative","archaic","rare"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"conj","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"You look a bit like my sister, except (that) she has longer hair.","type":"example"},{"text":"I never made fun of her except teasingly.","type":"example"},{"text":"To survive, I did everything except steal.","type":"example"},{"text":"Come any time except between ten and twelve.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used to introduce a clause, phrase, verb infinitive, adverb or other non-noun complement forming an exception or qualification to something previously stated."]},{"examples":[{"text":"They fired tear gas at us, except the wind was blowing the wrong way.","type":"example"},{"text":"I almost walked out, except I remembered the promise I had made.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Loosely, used to introduce a contrastive statement explaining why something wasn't successful, didn't happen, etc."],"tags":["informal"]},{"glosses":["Unless; used to introduce a hypothetical case in which an exception may exist."],"tags":["archaic"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ɪkˈsɛpt/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ɛkˈsɛpt/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ɪkˈsɛpt/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/ɛkˈsɛpt/","tags":["General-American","sometimes"]},{"ipa":"/əkˈsɛpt/"}],"upstream_line":236311,"upstream_offset":1161332945,"upstream_record_sha256":"8d462b5391c017f35cc6e3a93356067dab65791a5802ef629afefdc128a7bcce","word":"except"}
